# Extraction in Node (harness/run.mjs): gliner2.5-small-v1 through gliner-rs, Relate 1.3.0 WASM in memory
model fastino/gliner2.5-small-v1 @ 7132dc4, gliner-rs@4fa741e; every candidate at p >= 0.10 is cached and stored (D7)
each kind is asked by its label as text ("medical professional") with its relate-examples cue as the description (D22), each
link between its endpoints' label texts with its phrase ("claimant filed claim"); identifiers a kind's pattern proposes are
listed names (rule 1, D26); sections of about 3000 bytes
every question answered once, from the inference cache or the worker threads (harness/infer.mjs, D24); three answers asked again on this thread: identical
one store per ontology (D14): the 40 bundled ontologies (27 curated, 13 solution packages), each with its documents, and one for the harness's 12 fixtures
(harness/fixtures.mjs: edge cases, under create-context-graph's financial-services ontology as the page converts it); the fixtures,
then each ontology's documents (primary, then companions); each document is extracted, gated and published
default gate (store.defaultGate, gliner2.5-small-v1; every ontology): entity keep 0.700 drop 0.300; relation keep 0.200 drop 0.150;
  type margin 0.050; relation margin 0.050; pole margin 0.250
rules: 0 no-name (a pronoun or no canonical name), 1 gazetteer, 2 one-kind / type-margin / pole-specific, 2b other-kind,
       3 unanchored / end-in-review, 4 illegal / self-loop, 5 band / hedged, 6 ambiguous / reversed, 7 verdict / claim-verdict.
       A line: band, rule, sentence (s<idx>), then
       "text" (KIND) p or "head" -[REL]-> "tail" p
Each document is listed as the gate leaves its store after the last document (the gate runs per store).

They follow the cases edgextract's golden set covered, written for this domain; no gold. Listed: every mention and claim.
